订阅外层帧形如 `{"kind":"event","realm_id":"ak:realm:...","cursor":"<opaque>","payload":<上面 envelope>}`,详见 §2.3 `ak.self.events.stream.subscribe` 行与 [`client-sync.md`](./client-sync.md) §12。

跨域 actor 验证响应（通过 `/_arkret/root/identity/resolve` 与 holder-approved presentation challenge 获得）只能作为缓存加速或辅助诊断。接收方在接受事件、成员变更或设备绑定前，仍 MUST 独立验证 DID Document、key log、签名 transcript、capability 和 Realm policy；不得把对端"验证通过"当成最终授权依据。

#### 2.3.2 Private history-key recovery

| Operation | HTTP | Request | Response | Authorization / durability |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ak.self.history_key_requests.command.create` | `POST /_arkret/self/history-key-requests` | `history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_request` | `history_key_request_create_outcome` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_request`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_request_create_outcome`。当前 scope endpoint；operation envelope ≤ 1 MiB 且 inner request canonical bytes ≤ 64 KiB；release service 必须先从 caller-pinned bootstrap base 到 exact target 完成 direct Seal replay，验证独立 current anti-rollback basis 与 requester incarnation，并原子 pin 完整 traversal closure；request/receipt/response-stream/traversal retention expiry 逐字相等后才 durable accepted；exact retry 返回首次 bytes。 |
| `ak.self.history_key_requests.read.list` | `POST /_arkret/self/history-key-requests/read` | `history_key_request_list_query` | `history_key_request_list_outcome` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_request_list_query`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_request_list_outcome`。无状态 read-query；当前 scope member；RRK holder 仅交集 ranges 窄分支。 |
| `ak.self.history_key_responses.command.send` | `POST /_arkret/self/history-key-responses` | `history_key_response_send_request` | `history_key_response_send_receipt` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_response_send_request`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_response_send_receipt`。manifest 先全量 T0 admission；chunk 依赖其 digest 再做 current T1；首次 accepted 是 release 点，exact retry 返同一小 receipt。 |
| `ak.self.history_key_responses.read.list` | `POST /_arkret/self/history-key-responses/read` | `history_key_response_list_query` | `history_key_response_list_outcome` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_response_list_query`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_response_list_outcome`。无状态 read-query；`Authorization: Arkret-History-Capability <response_capability_b64u>` 是唯一 locator 与授权值，URL/query/body 均不携 request locator。 |
| `ak.self.history_key_responses.command.ack` | `POST /_arkret/self/history-key-responses/ack` | `history_key_response_ack_request` | `history_key_response_ack_outcome` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_response_ack_request`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_response_ack_outcome`。呈递同一 history response capability；只推进连续 durable dispositions。 |
| `ak.self.organization_recovery_archives.read.list` | `POST /_arkret/self/organization-recovery-archives/read` | `organization_recovery_archive_list_query` | `organization_recovery_archive_list_outcome` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/organization_recovery_archive_list_query`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/organization_recovery_archive_list_outcome`。holder-bound high-privilege recovery projection；query/cursor 绑定 provenance Event、完整 `holder_trusted_basis`、holder authority 及 last ordering tuple。每行必填 holder 首次 durable accepted replica 的 exact `archive_replica_digest` 与独立 exact-one-epoch archive-lifetime direct traversal retention；holder 必须把该 digest 原值写入 self Event/Seal/dependency resolve 的 `archive_replica_digest` access branch，不得以 archive digest、traversal intent digest 或 container Event ref 代替。holder 可读取重放所需全部 Control Move/Seal/dependency（可能含 membership/policy/control metadata），但不得读取无关 DataEvent、generic timeline scan 或取得 membership/send 权。不能接受该披露的部署不得启用 RRK durability。 |
| `ak.peer.history_key_requests.command.replicate` | `POST /_arkret/peer/history-key-requests/replicate` | `history_key_request_replica` | `history_key_request_replica_outcome` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_request_replica`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_request_replica_outcome`。私有 S2S request+receipt fanout；destination_authorization closed union 为 exact current member delivery binding 或与 ranges 相交的 exact RRK holder/archive tuple。 |
| `ak.peer.seals.read.mls_governance_proof` | `POST /_arkret/peer/seals/mls-governance-proof` | `read_request` | `read_outcome` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/mls-governance-proof-bundle.schema.json#/$defs/read_request`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/mls-governance-proof-bundle.schema.json#/$defs/read_outcome`。仅执行 near-current `group_security_frontier`，显式绑定 base/target Seal antichains；bulk/旧 epoch 不走此接口。 |
| `ak.peer.seals.read.governance_dependencies` | `POST /_arkret/peer/seals/governance-dependencies` | `PeerGovernanceDependencyResolveRequest` | `GovernanceDependencyResolveOutcome` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/PeerGovernanceDependencyResolveRequest`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/GovernanceDependencyResolveOutcome`。批量解析三种 exact typed evidence：AvailabilityReceipt、AuthenticatedSignerResolutionEvidence、MinimalMetadataMlsLeafSignerEvidence。省略 `history_traversal_access` 时走普通 authenticated Realm/federation visibility；仅 pending archive replica 可携其 exact digest 启用 retained-cut 窄例外，request replica 不授予该能力。unknown/out-of-cut/unauthorized 同形，完整响应超 byte limit 则零 partial 拒绝。 |
| `ak.peer.organization_recovery_archives.command.replicate` | `POST /_arkret/peer/organization-recovery-archives/replicate` | `organization_recovery_archive_replica` | `organization_recovery_archive_replica_outcome` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/organization_recovery_archive_replica`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/organization_recovery_archive_replica_outcome`。scope service 向 exact holder service 复制 archive、container ref 与独立 exact-one-epoch direct traversal retention；semantic tuple 唯一，改 replicated_at 不增 sequence。retained Control Move/Seal/dependency metadata 披露按 holder 高权限模型处理；不创建 ArchiveId/public Event/proof result/ak:snapshot。 |
| `ak.peer.history_key_responses.command.relay` | `POST /_arkret/peer/history-key-responses/relay` | `history_key_source_relay` | `history_key_response_send_receipt` | `request_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_source_relay`；`response_schema_ref=schemas/history-key.schema.json#/$defs/history_key_response_send_receipt`。source local PS 签 `SourceRelayAttestation` 并投递到 receipt 冻结的 release service；destination 执行唯一 T1 线性化并只返回 exact 小型 send receipt。 |

Request create 时，requester 当前 delivery binding/authenticated Principal Server 成为该 response stream 唯一 release service；receipt、
sealed capability HPKE context 与 response stream 均冻结其 service DID、binding/resolution refs 和 route digest。服务不得在 retry、fanout 或
head 变化时改选 release service。Request replica 与 source relay 只走上述两条私有 S2S carrier，不得映射为 public Event、
generic DeviceMessage 或通用 federation batch。双服务 cut 不是全局原子事务：v1 的边界是诚实服务、短 TTL、durable exact retry；
恶意或延迟 peer 不在密码学保证内。

以上 OpenAPI components MUST `$ref` canonical schema，不得内联复制。Unknown response stream、wrong/expired capability 与无权
scope list 返回同形 `not_found`。服务只持 capability commitment，constant-time 比较；capability 不得写入 cursor、log
或 response。Accepted request/record 在 durable disposition 前不得因容量压力驱逐；不可恢复损坏在同一 sequence stream 返回
service-signed lost descriptor。ack token 逐项绑定 normal/lost kind、sequence、response id 与对应 digest。

### 2.4 字段级 Schema 索引

本节是 REST 端点的字段级人类索引。字段写法为 `name: type - 说明`。出现在“必填字段”列的字段为该索引中的 required 摘要；出现在“可选字段”列的字段为 optional 摘要。位置若非 body，会显式标注为 `path.`、`query.` 或 `header.`。机器契约已存在时，“约束”列必须指向 `request_schema_ref` / `response_schema_ref`；完整字段集合、required 性、closed/open 状态以 JSON Schema 和 pipeline 生成的 [`operation-schema-index.json`](../../artifacts/reports/operation-schema-index.json) 为准。字段顺序按 operation DTO 顺序：target / identity 字段、scope 字段、auth/proof 字段、option 字段、body/content 字段、result 字段、audit/time 字段。

规范性 operation contract 以 `artifacts/registry/contract-registry.json#operation_registry` 为 canonical source；`artifacts/registry/operation-registry.json` 与 `artifacts/reports/operation-schema-index.json` 是其生成视图。本表、OpenAPI 与非 HTTP binding 均 MUST 从 canonical source 生成或通过 CI 校验；不得新增 catalog 中不存在的 `operation_id`，也不得在声明支持某 operation 时遗漏对应 catalog 条目。

#### 2.4.1 Binding completeness index

`binding_completeness` 由 operation registry 派生：声明 `request_schema_ref` / `response_schema_ref` 且 OpenAPI 指向同一 schema fragment 的 operation 为 `typed_schema`；绑定通用 `OperationRequest` / `OperationResult` body 的 operation MUST 声明 `generic_binding`，且 core-tier operation MUST NOT 使用 generic binding。

所有 v1 operation 均为 `typed_schema`。未列出的 operation 在 registry 中已经是 `typed_schema`、无 body 的 status response，或不使用 generic operation envelope。

| `operation_id` | 必填字段 | 可选字段 | 响应字段 | 约束 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ak.peer.erasure_receipt.command.submit` | `package: erasure_receipt_package` | `header.Idempotency-Key: string`（必填 transport header，不得出现在 body） | `status: enum(accepted,duplicate)`; `receipt_id: id`; `acceptance: erasure_receipt_acceptance` | request_schema_ref=schemas/erasure-receipt-operations.schema.json#/$defs/erasure_receipt_submit_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/erasure-receipt-operations.schema.json#/$defs/erasure_receipt_submit_outcome。Receiver 在写入前验证 receipt 闭包、issuer authority、全部 proofs、digest 与 exact retained stub；同 source/destination/key + 同 canonical body 逐字重放，异 body 零写入冲突。 |
| `ak.peer.erasure_receipt.resource.get` | `path.receipt_id: id` | 无 | `package: erasure_receipt_package` | response_schema_ref=schemas/erasure-receipt-operations.schema.json#/$defs/erasure_receipt_resource。只允许 issuer、receiver 或授权 auditor；未知、不可见与无权访问统一 `not_found`；retained stub 不得重建。 |
| `ak.peer.service_resolution.command.publish` | `header.Source-Service-ID: did_core_id`; `header.Destination-Service-ID: did_core_id`; `header.Content-Digest: digest`; `header.Signature-Input: string`; `header.Signature: string`; `header.Idempotency-Key: string`; `request_id: ak:request:<uuidv7>`; `realm_id: realm_id`; `artifact_digest: sha256`; exact-one `service_resolution_record: ServiceResolutionRecord` 或 `service_route_handover_notice: ServiceRouteHandoverNotice` | 无 | `ack: service_resolution_publish_ack {ack: {request_id, source_service_id, receiver_service_id, realm_id, request_digest, artifact_key, artifact_digest, accepted_at}, proof}` | request_schema_ref=schemas/identity-resolution.schema.json#/$defs/service_resolution_publish_request; response_schema_ref=schemas/identity-resolution.schema.json#/$defs/service_resolution_publish_outcome。HTTP `Idempotency-Key` MUST 逐字等于 body `request_id`；service signature 覆盖 method/target/content digest/source/destination/idempotency key。transport key `(source_service_id,realm_id,request_id)` 绑定 request digest，artifact key `(source_service_id,realm_id,artifact_key)` 绑定 artifact digest；任一异 digest 零覆盖 `duplicate_conflict`。notice request 不携 chain，落后 basis 必须先逐 record 独立 publish 并取得 durable ack。 |
| `ak.peer.service_resolution.read.resolve` | `header.Source-Service-ID: did_core_id`; `header.Destination-Service-ID: did_core_id`; `header.Content-Digest: digest`; `header.Signature-Input: string`; `header.Signature: string`; `realm_id: realm_id`; `target_service_id: did_core_id`; `target_service_kind: string`; `known_record_sequence: int`; `known_record_digest: sha256` | `known_notice_digest: sha256`; `max_records: int[1..32]`; `max_response_bytes: int[4096..262144]` | `successor_records: ServiceResolutionRecord[0..32]`; `handover_notice: ServiceRouteHandoverNotice?`; `has_more: boolean` | request_schema_ref=schemas/identity-resolution.schema.json#/$defs/service_resolution_resolve_request; response_schema_ref=schemas/identity-resolution.schema.json#/$defs/service_resolution_resolve_outcome。HTTP binding 使用 `QUERY`，不得携 `Idempotency-Key`；service signature 覆盖 method/target/content digest/source/destination。response 从 known basis 严格连续，canonical bytes ≤256 KiB；`successor_records=[]` 时 `has_more` MUST 为 false。认证/peer/visibility gate 失败统一 `capability_denied`，通过 gate 后 unknown/invisible/not-held/gap/fork/cancelled/expired 统一 `not_found`，具体诊断只进私有审计。 |
| `ak.server.read.describe` | 无 | `query.service_kind: string - 过滤服务类型` | `ServiceDescribe` | response_schema_ref=schemas/service-describe.schema.json。`public_metadata`; 不得返回私有拓扑或 secret。`trust_domain` 与 `plaintext_visibility` 必填；缺失时 callers MUST fail closed。 |
| `ak.root.identity.registry.read.describe` | 无 | 无 | `ServiceDescribe` | response_schema_ref=schemas/service-describe.schema.json。`public_metadata`; 可限流；identity-specific 字段只能作为扩展字段追加。 |
| `ak.root.identity.read.resolve` | `did: did - 待解析 DID` | `requested_evidence_kinds: enum(did_webvh)[]` | `did_document: object`; `key_log_head: id?`; `seq: int?`; `receipts: object[]?`; `method_evidence: closed did_webvh {version_id: non-empty, log_head_digest: sha256, control_key_digest: sha256}?` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/IdentityResolveRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/IdentityResolveOutcome。`did_webvh` evidence 只从完整已验证 WebVH history、exact head 与 active `parameters.updateKeys[0]` 派生；requested 但不可得时 fail closed。private DID 可要求 presentation proof；`ak.profile.ephemeral_pairwise_principal.v1` 的 did:key 只允许 client 本地 adapter 展开，MUST NOT 发送到网络 resolver、目录或该 endpoint。 |
| `ak.root.identity.document.resource.get` | `query.did: did` | `query.version: string - 指定版本或 head` | `did_document: object`; `head_event_digest: string?`; `seq: int?`; `receipts: object[]?` | response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/IdentityDocumentView。可见性同 `ak.root.identity.read.resolve`；Realm-local pairwise did:key 禁止网络查询。 |
| `ak.root.identity.log.read.list` | `query.did: did` | `query.cursor: cursor`; `query.limit: int` | `events: object[]`; `next_cursor: cursor?`; `has_more: boolean` | response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/IdentityLogListOutcome。private DID MUST 要求 holder-approved proof；Realm-local pairwise did:key 没有 method history 且禁止调用该网络 surface。 |
| `ak.root.identity.command.submit_did_operation` | `did: did`; `did_method: string`; `operation: object` | `seq: int`; `prev_event_digest: string` | `status: enum(accepted,duplicate,pending)`; `did: did`; `head_event_digest: string?`; `seq: int?`; `operation_ref: string?`; `receipts: object[]?` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/DidOperationSubmitRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/DidOperationSubmitOutcome。`did_method` 不含 `did:` 前缀且必须匹配 DID；`operation` 必须携带完整 method-native control proof，统一 wrapper 不得另造授权 proof 或降格为通用 `patch`；transport auth 不替代控制权。幂等键由 native operation id / seq / canonical bytes 决定；相同标识不同内容、stale head 或 sibling fork 必须 fail closed。 |
| `ak.root.identity.service_registration.command.ensure` | `service_kind: enum(principal_server,auth_server,identity_registry)`; `public_base: canonical-url`; `full_id: full_id`; `inception_operation: ServiceWebvhInceptionOperation`; `idempotency_key: string` | `previous_receipt: ServiceRegistrationReceipt or null` | `did_document: ServiceDidDocument`; `registration_receipt: ServiceRegistrationReceipt`; `created: boolean` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/ServiceRegistrationEnsureRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/ServiceRegistrationOutcome。`service_id`、`full_id` 与 `version_id` 只从 document/receipt 派生；`full_id == inception_operation.state.id`，且 adapter MUST 得到 `service_id=project(full_id)`。幂等对象是 canonical `(service_kind, public_base)`；Provider MUST 以数据库唯一约束和同事务先查后建保证并发安全，且必须扫描现存托管 document 阻止 mapping 丢失后的第二 DID。receipt 的 provider proof 以 signing-time provider `full_id` 的 verification method 验证，再由 adapter 投影并比对 `provider_service_id: did_core_id`。 |
| `ak.root.identity.service_registration.resource.get` | `query.service_kind: enum(principal_server,auth_server,identity_registry)`; `query.public_base: canonical-url` | 无 | 同 `ServiceRegistrationOutcome`，`created=false` | response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/ServiceRegistrationOutcome。只读诊断；不得隐式 ensure。 |
| `ak.open.identity.read.resolution` | `path.principal_id: did_core_id`; `query.principal_server_id: did_core_id` | 无 | `PublicPrincipalResolution` | response_schema_ref=schemas/identity-resolution.schema.json#/$defs/public_principal_resolution。public projection，不授权；`principal_id + principal_server_id` 是唯一 selector，也是完整外部 identity。响应只含 `resolution_projection`、bounded `method_history_evidence` 与 Principal Server 签名的 `projection_attestation`；MUST NOT 含 `principal_control_realm_id`、PCR genesis Event/receipt、resolution Event 或 accepted Seal，因此本面没有 history selector。消费方先验 `principal_server_id` 的 service route，再验 attestation proof、`project(full_id)==principal_id` 与 bounded method history；一组无证明裸字段不是合法响应。 |
| `ak.self.identity.read.resolution_audit` | body `principal_authority {principal_id,principal_server_id}` | `history_depth: int?`（closed `0..256`，默认 `0`）; `after_resolution_event_ref: event_ref?` | `PrincipalResolutionAuditEvidence` | request_schema_ref=schemas/identity-resolution.schema.json#/$defs/principal_resolution_audit_request; response_schema_ref=schemas/identity-resolution.schema.json#/$defs/principal_resolution_audit_evidence。账号内部审计面，承载 `principal_control_realm_id`、genesis Event/receipt、current 与 bounded predecessor resolution Event、accepted Seal；verifier 用统一 reducer 从 current Event 重放 current projection，不另造 resolution 专用 proof。授权只来自绑定该 exact `principal_authority` 的 current holder session；recovery actor 先完成既有 recovery transaction 成为 current holder，不另开第二条审计授权路径。unknown 账号、错误 authority pair 与无权调用者共用同一反枚举结果。`history_depth` 越界或与 `after_resolution_event_ref` 组合非法为 `param_invalid`；ancestor 不在当前 lineage 为 `param_invalid`（reason `resolution_history_ancestor_unknown`）。截断时 `history_complete=false` 并返回 `next_audit_cursor`。 |
| `ak.open.service.read.resolution` | `path.service_id: did_core_id` | 无 | 当前 `AuthenticatedServiceResolution` | response_schema_ref=schemas/identity-resolution.schema.json#/$defs/authenticated_service_resolution。`GET /_arkret/open/services/{service_id}/resolution`；public metadata，只发布 resolution，不授权。响应同时携带 signed `ServiceResolutionRecord`、exact method-history evidence 与 normalized DID document；`did:webvh` evidence **MUST** 是从 inception 到 record 位置的完整无缺口 native log，并携带所有适用 witness records，不得是摘要或部分区间。客户端独立验证 service id / kind / projection、route-binding digest、canonical base URL、时间序、完整 method history、witness、terminal document digest 与签名后，才可使用其中的 verification method 或冻结 Realm notary signer。canonical bytes ≤ 1 MiB、无 `Content-Encoding`、无 redirect；裸 `ServiceResolutionRecord`、history summary 或未验证的 current DID Document 不构成历史 signer evidence。 |
| `ak.root.identity.organization_registration.command.prepare` | `organization_id: did_core_id`; `full_id: full_id`; `local_admin_subject: did_core_id`; `requested_scopes: enum(organization_profile_manage,organization_realm_endorse,organization_service_delegate)[]` | 无 | `challenge_id: string`; `organization_id: did_core_id`; `full_id: full_id`; `purpose: const(ak.organization_registration_control_proof.v1)`; `nonce: string`; `audience: did_core_id`; `origin: canonical-url`; `trust_domain: string`; `local_admin_subject: did_core_id`; `requested_scopes: string[]`; `expires_at: datetime`; `created_at: datetime` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/OrganizationRegistrationChallengeRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/OrganizationRegistrationChallenge。registry MUST 独立验证 `full_id` 并要求 `project(full_id) == organization_id`；challenge 同时固定 core、full、受益管理员、scope 与 ≤300 秒窗口。 |

| `ak.root.identity.receipts.read.list` | `query.did: did`; `query.head: string` | 无 | `receipts: object[]`（tagged union，按 `schema` 常量分支：`ak.schema.identity_receipt.v1` 或 `ak.schema.did_webvh_witness_receipt.v1`）; `threshold_met: boolean?` | response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/IdentityReceiptListOutcome。只公开最小 witness receipt。`receipts[]` 是两个不同对象族的 tagged union：`ak.schema.identity_receipt.v1` 描述 DID registry consensus group 中的复制角色（`witness_role ∈ {writer, witness, replica}`，绑定 `seq` + `head_event_digest`），`ak.schema.did_webvh_witness_receipt.v1` 描述 did:webvh method witness 在某 `versionId` 上的观测（绑定 `version_id` + witness `did:key` + controlling organization + `observed_at`）。二者用同一个英文词表达不同含义，consumer MUST 按 `schema` 常量分支，MUST NOT 依据"哪些可选字段恰好出现"推断语义。`threshold_met` 依 [`../identity/identity-did.md` §3.4.2](../identity/identity-did.md) 的生效 policy（method-native threshold 与 deployment policy 的最严格交集）计算，缺席 MUST NOT 被读作 `true`；它是 Arkret 层便利信号，MUST NOT 替代对标准 `did-witness.json` proof 的验证。 |

| `ak.root.identity.recovery_session.command.create` | body `create_request`，含必填 `request_id` 与 closed `principal_authority {principal_id,principal_server_id}`；旧顶层 `principal_id` hard reject | `recovery_session` SessionGrant + matching DPoP；无 service-signature alternative | `session` | request_schema_ref=schemas/recovery-session.schema.json#/$defs/recovery_session_create_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/recovery-session.schema.json#/$defs/recovery_session_state。`principal_authority.principal_server_id` MUST 等于当前 authenticated server DID；server 以 exact pair 选择唯一 lifetime PCR lineage，unknown/wrong-service/mismatch fail closed。session state 与全部 proof transcript 绑定同一 pair、grant id 与 `cnf.jkt`；其余 model/generation/policy/frontier 只从 accepted PCR state snapshot，不能由请求方自报。签发 256-bit one-time challenge，TTL 为 `min(900s, grant remaining lifetime)`。 |

| `ak.find.directory.read.private_contact_discovery` | `profile: const ak.private_contact_discovery.v1`; `phase: enum(blind,match)`; `batch_id: ak:batch:<uuidv7>`; `key_epoch: int`; phase=blind 另需 `ciphersuite: const ristretto255-SHA512`、`blinded_elements: base64url(32-byte ristretto element)[batch_item_count]`；phase=match 另需 `derived_prefixes: base64url(16-byte)[batch_item_count]` | （无可选字段） | phase=blind：`evaluated_elements: base64url(32-byte)[batch_item_count]`; `evaluation_proofs: base64url(64-byte)[1]`; `derived_prefix_bytes: const 16`; `padding: ASCII-SP string`；phase=match：`hit_bitmap: boolean[batch_item_count]`; `handoff_stubs: object[batch_item_count]?`; `padding: ASCII-SP string` | request_schema_ref=schemas/directory-operations.schema.json#/$defs/directory_private_contact_discovery_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/directory-operations.schema.json#/$defs/directory_private_contact_discovery_outcome。Advertise 本操作必须提供 `ServiceDescribe.private_contact_discovery`；profile 固定 RFC 9497 modeVOPRF(0x01) + suite `ristretto255-SHA512`、单条 batched DLEQ proof 与固定 16-byte prefix。客户端发送前 dummy-pad 到 describe 的精确 batch_item_count，所有两轮数组等长同序。首次 blind 原子扣 quota 并把 device-scoped batch_id 绑定到 JCS request digest；完全相同重试返回缓存 outcome、不重扣，异 body 返回 409 `duplicate_conflict`；未知 / wrong-device / expired batch 统一 410 `psi_batch_unavailable`，pinned epoch 保留完整 completion TTL。429 quota 与 403 batch policy 使用带 padding 的 Class B ErrorEnvelope；200 / Class B body 使用 RFC 8785 JCS，Content-Length 必须恰好命中 phase bucket，省略 Content-Encoding、设置 no-transform，并使用 describe 的同一 delay sampler。逐目标情形仅编码为 `hit_bitmap=false`（Class A）；handoff field shape 由 describe 固定 always / never。详见 [`../discovery/discovery-directory.md` §6.2–§6.4](../discovery/discovery-directory.md)。 |

| `ak.self.blob.upload.create` | `content: binary`; `size_bytes: int` | `realm_id: id`; `content_digest: string`; `media_type: string`; `filename: string`; `purpose: string` | `blob_ref: string`; `size_bytes: int`; `media_type: string?`; `content_digest: string`; `upload_receipt: object?` | request_schema_ref=schemas/blob-operations.schema.json#/$defs/blob_upload_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/blob-operations.schema.json#/$defs/blob_upload_outcome。upload capability、quota、media policy；`content` part Content-Type 缺省为 `application/octet-stream`。`size_bytes` 可由客户端声明，也可由服务端在响应中按实际接收字节计算后返回；二者不一致时 MUST `digest_mismatch` 或 `param_invalid`。 |

| `ak.self.blob.command.presign` | `blob_ref: string` | `max_age_seconds: int (<=3600)`; `purpose: enum(media_inline, thumbnail, download)` | `url: uri`; `expires_at: timestamp`; `purpose: string` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/BlobPresignRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/BlobPresignOutcome。为单个 blob 签发短 TTL（默认 ≤ 5 min，硬上限 ≤ 1h）、单对象、只读、可撤销的 pre-signed URL。**仅用于让浏览器 `<img src>` / `<video src>` 等无法附 Authorization header 的原生标签渲染受保护媒体**。E2EE 附件 ciphertext MUST NOT 通过此机制下发。受 `ak.self.blob.command.presign` capability 控制；TTL / scope / purpose 由 grant constraint 收紧。详见 [`crypto-media/media-and-blob.md` §5.4](../crypto-media/media-and-blob.md)。 |
| `ak.edge.push.command.register_device` | `device_id: id`; `push_gateway: url`; `push_key: string` | `platform: string`; `app_id: string`; `display_name: string`; `recipient_service_id: did_core_id` | `ok: boolean`; `push_target_id: PushTargetId`; `registration_id: id?`; `expires_at: datetime?` | request_schema_ref=schemas/push-operations.schema.json#/$defs/push_register_device_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/push-operations.schema.json#/$defs/push_register_device_outcome。只能注册当前 principal/device，且 registration 作用域绑定当前 Principal Server service `did_core_id`；如显式携带 `recipient_service_id`，MUST 等于目标服务的 `did_core_id`。响应的 `push_target_id` 是服务端按 `discovery/push-notifications.md` §2.2 派生的 pairwise pseudonym，notify 调用方 MUST 使用该值。 |
| `ak.edge.push.command.unregister_device` | `device_id: id` | `push_key: string`; `app_id: string` | `ok: boolean` | request_schema_ref=schemas/push-operations.schema.json#/$defs/push_unregister_device_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/push-operations.schema.json#/$defs/push_unregister_device_outcome。same device/principal 或 device revocation path。 |
| `ak.edge.push.command.notify` | `notification.push_target_id: PushTargetId`; `notification.wakeup_kind: enum(message,mention,assignment,schedule,reaction,call_invite,reminder,scheduled_send,expiry_invalidation)`; `notification.timing_profile_hint: enum(default,traffic_metadata_hardened)`; `notification.devices: object[]` | `header.Idempotency-Key: string`; `notification.push_hint: string`; `notification.counts: object`; routing-stripped（gateway-internal，出 provider 前 strip）: `notification.timing_profile_hint: string`, `notification.route_tokens: object {realm_route_token?, scope_route_token?, delivery_binding_frontier_token?}``; 顶层: `event_kind: string`, `reason_code: string`, `audit_envelope: object {access_kind, late_recovery_original_event_id?}`; visible profile only: `notification.event_id: id`, `notification.realm_id: id`, `notification.sender_actor_id: did_core_id`, `notification.strand_id: id`, `notification.message_id: id`, `notification.sender_actor_display_name: string`, `notification.strand_title: string`, `notification.realm_title: string`, `notification.user_is_target: boolean`, `notification.priority: string`, `notification.membership: string` | `push_target_id: PushTargetId`; `outcomes: object[] {device_id, gateway_status: enum(accepted,duplicate,rejected), reason_code?: enum(push_target_unknown,push_payload_too_large,unsupported_profile,delivery_binding_stale,push_token_unknown,push_token_invalid,push_gateway_unreachable,rate_limited), retry_after_ms?}` | request_schema_ref=schemas/push-operations.schema.json#/$defs/push_notify_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/push-operations.schema.json#/$defs/push_notify_outcome。响应对 `notification.devices[]` 逐项守恒：每个输入 `device_id` 在 `outcomes[]` 中恰好出现一次，不得遗漏、重复或出现未请求的 device；target 级失败 MUST 展开为每个输入 device 一条同 `reason_code` 的 `rejected`，不存在第二种响应形状。`accepted` / `duplicate` 表示 gateway 已 durable 接管，调用方 MUST NOT 因后续 provider 失败重发；只有 `rejected` 且带 `retry_after_ms` 时调用方才拥有下一次尝试。provider 投递状态、provider message id、原始 push token 及其 hash MUST NOT 出现在本响应中。来自授权 Sync 或 notification service；`idempotency_key` / `origin_service_id` / `destination_service_id` / `recipient_service_id` 走 transport header（`Idempotency-Key` / `Source-Service-ID` / `Destination-Service-ID`，后者由 `recipient_service_id` 复用），`operation_id` 由 path 唯一确定，均不入 body。默认 blind wakeup 请求 MUST NOT 携带 event / realm / sender 字段；独立第三方 Push Gateway 的路由输入只能是 opaque token，且不得承载 raw Realm id、Circle id、`effective_scope` 或 actor DID allow-list。`notification.timing_profile_hint=traffic_metadata_hardened` 表示来源服务已在 ServiceDescribe 声明支持并由部署配置对该 route 应用 `ak.profile.traffic_metadata_hardened.v1`；它只用于 gateway provider-visible timing bucket，不进入 provider payload，也不构成 Realm profile activation。visible 字段只在 `ak.profile.push_gateway.visible_notification.v1` 且 Realm policy + device opt-in + UI disclosure 通过时允许；`notification.content`、`body`、`preview`、`summary` 或任意 `content_*` 仍然禁止。`service_signature` 的 transcript MUST 绑定 `push_target_id` 所对应 registration 的 registration service DID（即 `ak.edge.push.command.register_device` 注册作用域绑定的 Principal Server service DID）；且发起 notify 的来源服务 DID MUST 出现在该设备 registration 的 `recipient_service_id` 投递链内。push gateway MUST 拒绝来源服务不在目标设备投递链内、或 transcript 未绑定该 registration service DID 的请求（`capability_denied`），以阻止跨 Realm / 跨 Principal Server 的 push 注入。 |
| `ak.self.device_messages.command.send` | `header.Idempotency-Key: string`; `messages: object` | 每个 target 必须含 sender-assigned `device_message_id: ak:device_message:<uuidv7>`、`kind`、`content`、`expires_at` | `ok: boolean`; `delivered: object?`; `unknown_devices: object?` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/DeviceMessagesSendRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/DeviceMessagesSendOutcome。`(sender, Idempotency-Key)` 约束 HTTP request replay；客户端 device 分支按 `(sender_principal_id, sender_device_id, device_message_id)` 约束逻辑消息重试，服务端 MUST 把 `device_message_id` 原样复制进 `DeviceMessageEnvelope`，同 ID 不同 canonical target intent MUST `duplicate_conflict`（reason `device_message_id_conflict`）。该客户端 operation MUST 拒绝 `sender_service_id`；service sender 只由 Principal Server 内部 materializer 产生，且只能承载 closed actor-private update kind。发送方与目标都必须是已授权 device；未接受设备必须先走匿名 pairing，不能调用 self surface；缺失 `device_message_id`、过期或超过 TTL 上限的消息必须拒绝或逐项 reject。 |
| `ak.self.device_messages.read.list` | 无 | `query.after: cursor`; `query.limit: int` | `messages: object[]`; `ack_token: string?`; `next_cursor: cursor?`; `has_more: boolean`; `limited: boolean?`; `lost: boolean?` | response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/DeviceMessagesGetOutcome。响应字段 `messages[]` 含 `DeviceMessageEnvelope` 对象, 不是 Event Envelope; 只返回当前 device 队列；`after=` 是只读位置，不触发队列删除；`messages` 非空时 MUST 返回 `ack_token`。 |
| `ak.self.device_messages.command.ack` | `ack_token: string` | 无 | `ok: boolean`; `pruned_count: int?` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/DeviceMessagesAckRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/DeviceMessagesAckOutcome。显式 to-device 投递确认（[`client-sync.md` §10.1](./client-sync.md)）：累计单调、天然幂等；unknown / 过期 / cross-binding 令牌返回 `param_invalid`（reason `invalid_ack_token`）且不得删除任何消息。 |

| `ak.gate.account.command.issue_identity_binding_challenge` | `request_id: id`; `identity_creation_lease_id: string`; `lease_fence: int`; `did_operation: DidOperationSubmitRequestBody`; `full_id: full_id`; `pcr_realm_id: id`; `realm_create_payload_digest: hash`; `founding_authorize_payload_digest: hash`; `initial_session_request_digest: hash` | 无 | `request_id: id`; `challenge_id: string`; `challenge: string`; `purpose: const(account_binding_and_pcr_genesis)`; `account_subject: hash`; `principal_id: did_core_id`; `full_id: full_id`; `operation_digest: hash`; `did_version_id: string`; `log_head_digest: hash`; `control_key_digest: hash`; `pcr_realm_id: id`; `realm_create_payload_digest: hash`; `founding_authorize_payload_digest: hash`; `initial_session_request_digest: hash`; `genesis_unit_kinds: const[]`; `identity_creation_lease_id: string`; `lease_fence: int`; `dpop_jkt: string`; `audience: did_core_id`; `origin: uri`; `trust_domain: id`; `issued_at: datetime`; `expires_at: datetime` | request_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/identity_binding_challenge_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/identity_binding_challenge_outcome。完整 operation 进入 holder-authenticated checkpoint（非公开）；challenge 必须服务端生成、共享持久化、单次使用、≤300 秒。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.issue_did_binding_challenge` | `request_id: id`; `principal_id: did_core_id`; `full_id: full_id` | 无 | `request_id: id`; `challenge_id: string`; `challenge: string`; `purpose: const(account_binding_for_published_did)`; `account_subject: string`; `principal_id: did_core_id`; `full_id: full_id`; `did_version_id: string`; `log_head_digest: hash`; `control_key_digest: hash`; `witness_evidence: string`; `dpop_jkt: string`; `audience: did_core_id`; `origin: uri`; `trust_domain: id`; `issued_at: datetime`; `expires_at: datetime` | request_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/did_binding_challenge_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/did_binding_challenge_outcome。用于绑定**已发布**的 DID：调用方同时给出稳定 `principal_id` 与可解析 `full_id`，Account Authority MUST 自行按 `high` freshness tier 解析 `full_id`（同步刷新或 fail closed），由注册 method adapter 要求 `project(full_id)=principal_id`，并从解析出的 current entry 推导三项 DID pin，MUST NOT 接受调用方自报的 DID history。不自托管该 DID 的部署 MUST 另行要求 method-native witness / freshness 证据——接受 registry 与 Principal Server 同源**不外延**到第三方 DID。challenge 必须服务端生成、共享持久化、单次使用、≤300 秒，并绑定 `account_subject`、`principal_id`、`full_id`、holder jkt、三项 DID pin、audience、origin、trust domain、purpose 与 expiry。本操作不保留 principal、不执行 PCR genesis，只证明对既有 DID 的当前控制权。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.issue_identity_abandonment_challenge` | `request_id: id`; `identity_creation_lease_id: string`; `lease_fence: int`; `principal_id: did_core_id`; `did_version_id: string` | 无 | `request_id: id`; `challenge_id: string`; `challenge: string`; `purpose: const(provisional_identity_abandonment)`; `account_subject: hash`; `principal_id: did_core_id`; `did_version_id: string`; `identity_creation_lease_id: string`; `lease_fence: int`; `consequence_disclosure: const[]`; `dpop_jkt: string`; `audience: did_core_id`; `origin: uri`; `trust_domain: id`; `issued_at: datetime`; `expires_at: datetime` | request_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/identity_abandonment_challenge_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/identity_abandonment_challenge_outcome。凭据只能是 account handoff grant：PCR 从未 accepted，用户没有 principal，也就没有绑定 principal 的 `ak.session.grant`。`principal_id` / `did_version_id` MUST 等于 holder-authenticated `reserved_identity_creation` checkpoint；PCR 已 accepted MUST 以 `identity_creation_already_accepted` 拒绝。challenge 必须服务端生成、共享持久化、单次使用、≤300 秒，expired 与 consumed 是不同终态。`consequence_disclosure` 是必须展示的封闭后果集合，不是客户端自报回执。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.abandon_identity_creation` | `request_id: id`; `challenge_id: string`; `challenge: string`; `identity_creation_lease_id: string`; `lease_fence: int`; `principal_id: did_core_id`; `did_version_id: string` | 无 | `request_id: id`; `status: const(abandoned)`; `account_subject: hash`; `principal_id: did_core_id`; `did_version_id: string`; `abandoned_at: datetime` | request_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/identity_abandonment_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/identity_abandonment_outcome。MUST 出示一份新鲜 handoff grant，MUST NOT 复用签发 challenge 时那份；重认证强度、风险检查项与冷却期时长属部署治理，本规范不定义。消费 challenge、写 orphan anchor tombstone/audit reservation、在所有 holder 可读面抑制 `reserved_identity_creation`、释放 lease 必须同一事务，任一步失败零写入；同 `request_id` 重放返回同一终态且只有一条 tombstone。challenge 与确认之间 PCR 被 accepted 时 MUST `identity_creation_already_accepted` 且 MUST NOT 放弃。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.register` | `principal_id: did_core_id`; `full_id: full_id` | `display_name?`; `proof?`; `identity_creation: {lease_id,lease_fence,did_operation,registration_did_evidence_draft,control_proof,pcr_genesis_unit,initial_session}` | `AccountRegisterOutcome` + `pcr_genesis_receipt?` + `session_grant_outcome?` | `proof` 与 `identity_creation` 至少且仅一个；两分支均验证 `project(full_id)=principal_id`。draft 的专用 control proof 与 `identity_creation_control_proof` 必须分别验证；registry 接受 exact operation 后由 Account Authority 加入稳定 `accepted_at`。account-first saga `reserved -> did_published -> pcr_accepted -> account_bound -> completed`；`initial_session_request_digest`、RFC7638 JKT、founding device、audience/scope ceiling必须一致。 request_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/account_register_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/account_register_outcome。 |
| `ak.peer.principal_genesis.command.submit` | `account_authority_id`; `principal_id`; `pcr_realm_id`; `idempotency_key`; `registration_request_digest`; `registration_did_operation`; `registration_did_evidence`; `identity_creation_control_proof`; `genesis_unit` | 无 | `principal_id`; `pcr_realm_id`; `accepted_device_id`; `receipt` | Principal Server MUST only replay the relayed method-native operation and frozen evidence, MUST NOT reconstruct from current resolution；receipt `scope.registration_evidence_digest` reverse-binds the complete evidence. second Event proof 的 `verification_method` 固定为 candidate `full_id` 下 fragment 等于 `device_id` 的完整 DID URL（不得把 `principal_id` 直接拼 fragment），通过 candidate overlay 验签。 request_schema_ref=schemas/principal-operations.schema.json#/$defs/pcr_genesis_submit_request; response_schema_ref=schemas/principal-operations.schema.json#/$defs/pcr_genesis_submit_outcome。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.issue_session_grant` | human: `request_id,principal_id,device_id,audience,accepted_device_possession_proof`; Agent: `principal_id,device_id,requested_scope,agent_* fields,proof(agent_key_proof)` | 无 | `SessionGrantOutcome` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/SessionGrantRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/SessionGrantOutcome。closed human/Agent union；human 用 AccountHandoff Authorization DPoP，禁带 scope/body-holder-signature/OIDC，origin `returning_session_grant_issue` 同锁验证 accepted-device PoP + current authorization；仅 allow 签含 `device_binding` 的固定-scope Standard grant。Agent 保留 required scope 与独立 verifier。exact replay byte-identical。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.revoke_session` | 无 | `target_session_grant_id: id`; `target_device_id: id`; `all_sessions: true`; `applet_id: id`; `effective_scope: object`; `registration_epoch: hash`; `service_id: did_core_id`; `capability_grant_refs: ref[]`; `proof: AccountLifecycleProof` | `revoked_count: int`; `revoked_session_grant_ids?: id[]` | request_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/session_revoke_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/session_revoke_outcome。空 body 撤当前 session；grant/device/all_sessions/applet selector 互斥；跨 grant/device/all_sessions/applet 需要 fresh proof；不撤 device authorization。撤销必须用 issuer、operation kind、authenticated session/principal、canonical selector/body 派生稳定 request identity，并 durable 保存首次 mutation outcome；同 identity + 同 intent 在重验当前调用方授权后返回 byte-identical outcome，同 identity + 异 intent 返回 `duplicate_conflict` 且不再改变状态。account/device/Agent cascade 与 introspection 读取同一 issuer ledger，不发布已删除的 SessionGrant lifecycle Event 或其它 principal Event。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.refresh_session_grant` | `HumanSessionGrantRefreshRequest \| AgentSessionGrantRefreshRequest` | 无 | `SessionGrantRefreshOutcome` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/SessionGrantRefreshRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/SessionGrantRefreshOutcome。固定 Authorization DPoP；human accepted-device proof 与 Agent runtime proof 分支互斥，无 challenge/proof_kind；successor 原子继承 subject/scope/audience/cnf/session_id 并 supersede predecessor，exact replay 不产生第二 successor。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.issue_recovery_completion_grant` | `transaction_id: id`; `transaction_request_digest: hash`; `terminal_receipt: RecoveryReceipt`; `completion_attestation: RecoveryCompletionAttestation`; `device_authorization_event_id: id`; `result_model_generation_ref`; `initial_session: InitialSessionGrantIntent`; `canonical_request_digest: hash` | `Authorization: DPoP <account_handoff_grant>` + HTTP DPoP | `transaction_id`; `session_grant_outcome: SessionGrantOutcome`; `issued_at` | request_schema_ref=schemas/recovery-authority.schema.json#/$defs/issue_recovery_completion_grant_request; response_schema_ref=schemas/recovery-authority.schema.json#/$defs/issue_recovery_completion_grant_outcome。只接受仍有效且绑定该 account/principal 的 Bound AccountHandoff、coordinator-signed completed attestation、匹配 receipt 与当前 active replacement device；`initial_session.device_id` 必须等于 replacement device，session JWK 的 RFC 7638 thumbprint必须等于 handoff/HTTP DPoP JKT，audience/scope必须在 issuer ceiling 内；成功直接创建 `credential_class=standard` grant，不创建临时 recovery grant；相同 transaction + handoff + bytes返回 durable 首次 issuance。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.logout_auth_session` | `grant_jwt: string` | `logout_request_digest: string`; `validated_at: timestamp`; `reason_code: enum(account_logout)` | `ok: bool`; `grant_chain_terminated: bool`; `auth_session_logged_out: bool` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/AuthSessionLogoutRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/AuthSessionLogoutOutcome。Account Authority → Auth Server 的 S2S Auth-side logout 子操作；使用与 session-grant introspection 同族的部署内 S2S bearer；登出该 grant 所属 Auth-side session / `browser_session` 并终结 grant 轮换链。该操作幂等；已登出、已吊销、未知或已剪枝 grant/session 均按成功处理。普通客户端 MUST NOT 调用。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.logout` | `header.Authorization: bearer`; `header.DPoP: proof` | 无 | `ok: bool`; `revoked: bool` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/AccountLogoutRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/AccountLogoutOutcome。Account Authority 普通 hard logout 单入口：Authorization 出示当前 `ak.session.grant`，DPoP holder proof 绑定 grant 与 `/logout` endpoint；内部终结 Auth-side session / grant 轮换链与 Principal 本地 account session / 设备会话记录，并清该设备待投递 to-device / push registration；不同于 `revoke_session`（仅撤会话令牌）。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.request_erasure` | `request_id: id` | 无 | `request_id: id`; `status: enum(accepted)`; `principal_id: did_core_id`; `recorded_at: datetime`; `withdrawal_window_ends_at?: datetime` | request_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/account_request_erasure_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/account-operations.schema.json#/$defs/account_request_erasure_outcome。用户自助擦除唯一入口，由 Account Authority 在 gate 面直接受理；只鉴权 + durable 记录意图 + 在同一服务内继续既有 `erasure_pending` 签发流程，响应是受理确认而非完成回执；fresh 高风险动作认证由 Account Authority 本地判定，不满足时 `reauthentication_required` 零写入；exact replay 返回首次受理 outcome，同 key 异 bytes `duplicate_conflict`，存在未签发 record 的 live 意图时异 `request_id` 以 `failed_precondition`/`erasure_request_already_pending` 拒绝；record 签发后不可逆。完整 normative 语义见 [`../identity/account-lifecycle.md` §8.1](../identity/account-lifecycle.md#81-用户自助擦除入口normative)。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.introspect_session_grant` | 无 | `id: string`; `grant_jwt: string`; `audience: string`; `proof?: object` | `active: bool`; `status: string`; `proof_required: bool`; `one_time_use_consumed: bool`; `grant: object?` | request_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/SessionGrantIntrospectRequestBody; response_schema_ref=schemas/service-operation-dtos.schema.json#/$defs/SessionGrantIntrospectOutcome。RFC 7662 式 server-to-server 内省；`id` 与 `grant_jwt` 二选一；`session_grant_introspection_bearer` 或 admin scope 认证；**read-only**，MUST NOT 消费/吊销 grant。结果必须来自 issuer ledger 的 current active/revoked/superseded state，expired 从 immutable `expires_at` 判定；本地 cache 只能是 freshness-bounded projection。跨服务可转交 status 必须带 issuer/service signature；部署内直连至少经 authenticated channel 且绑定 exact request。`proof` 为 S2S 可选附加确认，不是普通客户端 self-path header；`grant.cnf_jkt` MUST 返回（供 Principal Server 校验 `/_arkret/self/*` 每请求 DPoP，见 [`api-conventions.md` §3.3](./api-conventions.md)）；`grant.session_public_key` 供 RFC 9421 PoP 校验；`browser_session` finished 后 MUST 返回 inactive。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.issue_controller_gate_attestation` | `request_id: string`; `principal_id: did_core_id`; `agent_authority_service_id: did_core_id`; `agent_authority_service_resolution: authenticated_service_resolution` | 无 | `request_id`; `controller_account_gate_attestation` | request_schema_ref=schemas/agent-signer-evidence-operations.schema.json#/$defs/controller_account_gate_attestation_issue_request; response_schema_ref=#/$defs/controller_account_gate_attestation_issue_outcome。仅 authenticated S2S；source service、字段值与 controller current `origin Principal Server admission proof` 的 Agent PCR authority 必须三方匹配。receiver先独立验 resolution carrier，再以其 active key验证覆盖method/target/path/digest/source/destination/operation/request-id的RFC9421；bearer不替代签名。Account Authority从自身权威projection签发≤300秒的closed lifecycle snapshot；unknown/unbound/wrong-service统一`not_found`。exact replay byte-identical，同id异intent `duplicate_conflict`。operation/request_digest/audience/challenge不进入本snapshot，由携其digest的per-request current observation + outer attestation完成exact绑定。不得返回或记录到portable outcome中的材料包括`account_id`与raw account cell。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.pair_device` | `pairing_code: string`; `new_device_pubkey: object`; `challenge_proof: object`; `device_pairing_request_id: string` XOR `challenge_transcript: object` | `display_name: string`; `device_metadata: object` | `device_id: id`; `authorized_event_ref: ref`; `device_grant: object?`; `key_backup_hint: object?` | request_schema_ref=schemas/agent-operations.schema.json#/$defs/account_device_pair_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/agent-operations.schema.json#/$defs/account_device_pair_outcome。pairing code / proof 必须短期有效、一次性使用，并绑定目标 Account Authority `gate_account_base` / origin / request canonical hash；`challenge_proof` 的唯一 signing transcript 与验签步骤见 [device-lifecycle.md §2.1.2](../crypto-media/device-lifecycle.md)，服务端 MUST 独立重算 transcript 验签，MUST NOT 只做逐字段相等比较；target `device_signature` 使用 `accepted_device` possession domain，服务端 MUST 用同一次重算得到的 `transcript_digest` 加 `authorize_event.event.payload` 的目标材料重建签名对象再验签（见 [device-lifecycle.md §5.2](../crypto-media/device-lifecycle.md) §5.2.2），请求体不承载 attestation 对象；服务端 MUST 按 principal、授权源设备和目标 origin 限速。携带 `device_pairing_request_id` 时 MUST 原子核验并消费匹配的 server-mediated 暂存记录。 |
| `ak.gate.account.command.pair_agent_key` | `agent_id: did_core_id`; `verification_method: did_url`; `public_key: object`; `proof_of_possession: agent_runtime_key_possession_proof`; `requested_scope_disclosure: AgentRequestedScopeDisclosure`; `authorize_event: EventInitialSubmission`; `signing_key_binding: AgentSigningKeyBinding`; `header.Idempotency-Key=authorize_event.event.event_id` | `runtime_attestation: object` | `ok: boolean`; `activation_state: enum(awaiting_accepted_frontier,active)`; `authorize_event_ref: ref`; `signing_key_binding: AgentSigningKeyBinding` | request_schema_ref=schemas/agent-operations.schema.json#/$defs/agent_key_pair_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/agent-operations.schema.json#/$defs/agent_key_pair_outcome。`requested_scope_disclosure` 是 verifier/audience/challenge-bound 私有 sidecar；MUST 验证 controller proof、单次 challenge、接收窗口与 accepted-at DID digest，且不得写入 `authorize_event` 或公开 history。`authorize_event.event` MUST 是 Agent PCR 中 `actor_id=agent_id`、`executed_by=controller`、带可验证 controller delegation 的完整签名 Event；在线 submission authority MUST 来自 typed request context；服务端不得要求额外 sidecar，也不得代签 receipt。其 `signing_key_binding_digest` MUST 承诺请求中的公开 binding，服务端 MUST 独立验证 binding controller proof、raw key digest、method、Event ID 与 validity。服务端还 MUST 从 current record 重建 `ak.agent_runtime_key_possession_proof.v1` 与含 current proof digest 的 `ak.agent.key_pairing_request_binding.v1`；body/current/proof/approval 任一不一致 fail closed。`verification_method` 的 bare full DID MUST 经注册 method adapter 投影为 `agent_id`；不得要求完整 DID 与 `did_core_id` 字节相等。相同 submission / pairing 重试幂等；同 Event ID 不同 Event bytes 或不同 typed request context 返回 conflict。首阶段 durable Event 返回 awaiting 且不激活；controller Seal 覆盖后重试，portable evidence verified 才返回 active。split Account Authority MUST 透明转发两阶段 outcome，并把完全相同的 typed request 委托到权威 Principal Server 的同一 canonical operation，MUST NOT 改用产品私有 fan-out endpoint。replacement re-pairing 时，controller-signed `authorize_event.event.payload.supersedes[]` MUST 精确列出全部既有 active authorization，reducer 接受该单一 Event 时原子替换并记录 reason=`superseded_by_repairing`。 |
| `ak.self.agent.command.provision` | prepare含`operation_id,idempotency_key,full_id,controller_principal_server_id,slug,requested_scope`；commit另含原`allocation_handle,agent_id,principal_control_realm_id`与恰好一个`provision_event: EventInitialSubmission`，且不重复 selector | prepare可含UI preview字段但不得canonical透传 | prepare返回accepted entry 0 的 exact `initial_resolution`、service-signed allocation 与 selector 精确锁定的delegation/controller-PCR坐标（`awaiting_controller_event`）；commit为`awaiting_pcr_genesis`；genesis accepted为`awaiting_did_binding`；连续 entry 1 accepted 后为`complete` | request_schema_ref=schemas/agent-operations.schema.json#/$defs/agent_provision_request_body; response_schema_ref=schemas/agent-operations.schema.json#/$defs/agent_provision_outcome。prepare 将 authenticated session principal 与 `controller_principal_server_id` 组成 exact `(principal_id, principal_server_id)` authority pair，逐字匹配本服务 durable account record，并只从该 pair 的本地唯一 PCR lineage 读取 controller 状态；只写private durable reservation。same operation/key/body回放，different body conflict。服务端必须保存exact inception承诺并在两条create admission路径逐字段比较。realm id只由controller从冻结create的event id派生；commit、genesis与entry 1必须分步且各自幂等。entry 1 accepted前Agent、pairing及list/get不可见；abandonment只适用于genesis尚未接受的显式放弃。public只含scope commitment，完整scope仅走绑定verifier/audience/challenge的private disclosure。display/avatar在complete后以独立profile Event提交。 |
